- **weighted-similarity-domain-matching**: Use when two sets of TAD/TLD boundary predictions must be compared quantitatively (e.g., noisy vs. reference Hi-C calls, or two predictors on the same matrix) and a contact-weighted domain-overlap score, excluding intermediate windows, is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/weighted-similarity-domain-matching))
- **weighted-similarity-score-compound-sampling**: Use when expanding a compound database with Pickaxe rules and unfiltered or similarity-cutoff expansion grows exponentially (e.g. YMDB one-generation expansion gives 575,262 compounds), but you need a fixed-size, target-biased network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-similarity-score-compound-sampling))
- **weighted-similarity-scoring**: Use when comparing TLD/TAD partitions predicted from a raw bulk Hi-C matrix against partitions from downsampled (e.g. 0.1%, 0.05%, 0.025%) or otherwise degraded Hi-C data to quantify predictor robustness to sparsity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/weighted-similarity-scoring))
- **weighted-sos-fermionic-representability**: Weighted-SOS witnesses for fermionic RDM representability. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/weighted-sos-fermionic-representability) · ★4)
- **weighted-trans-mediation-fraction-computation**: Use when a copy-number (SCNA) or epi-DNA alteration has both a cis gene candidate set and a trans gene expression signature, and you must rank cis genes by how much of the trans effect they statistically mediate to flag the Rank-1 driver. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/weighted-trans-mediation-fraction-computation))
- **weighted-unifrac-computation**: Use when you have a Qemistree chemical tree plus a feature table of MS1 peak areas and need tree-informed sample comparisons, e.g., to quantify how chemically similar foods or samples are. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-unifrac-computation))
- **weighted-unifrac-distance-computation**: Use when comparing feature/OTU tables generated by alternative DC methods (vsearch closed/open/de novo at 0.97, DADA2, Deblur) on 16S data, or benchmarking a method against a known mock-community composition on a reference phylogeny. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/weighted-unifrac-distance-computation))
- **weighted-unifrac-distance-estimation-on-chemical-tree**: Use when a metabolomics chemical-feature table (FeatureTable[Frequency]) is paired with a Qemistree hierarchy and you need tree-informed sample distances, PCoA ordination, or PERMANOVA that reduces chromatography-driven batch signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-unifrac-distance-estimation-on-chemical-tree))
- **weighted-unweighted-score-concordance-evaluation**: Use when a SMITE-style p-value aggregation offers optional per-interval weights (e.g., TSS distance) and you must decide whether weighting materially changes final gene scores, modules, or annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/weighted-unweighted-score-concordance-evaluation))
- **weighted-utr-isoform-usage-analysis**: Use when scUTRquant SingleCellExperiment outputs (gene + 3'UTR isoform count matrices) cover multi-UTR genes across two or more cell types or perturbations and differentially used/lengthened 3' ends must be called and compared to DGE gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-utr-isoform-usage-analysis))
- **weighted-vs-unweighted-artifact-comparison-visualization**: Use when metacell-level (rather than single-cell-level) differential expression, differential abundance, or dimensionality reduction is planned and metacells have unequal sizes, to quantify and visualize the type I/II errors introduced by disregarding metacell size weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-vs-unweighted-artifact-comparison-visualization))

- **weights-biases-api-rate-limit-hit-mid-12f7f498f7d235d7**: Pace histogram fetches like the heavy reads they are: histograms cost more than metadata on the API, so an agent fetching them run by run in a burst trips the limit mid-comparison. - **weights-biases-artifact-upload-failed-3d0e2901618ad3f8**: A 413 means the request body exceeded a size limit on the upload path: split the artifact into smaller files, compress genuinely compressible content, or raise the limit where you control the proxy or self-hosted server. Use when a wandb artifact upload of a large model or dataset snapshot. Not for a small artifact failing with 413 because a proxy limit is set near zero by mistake, where the fix is the proxy. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/weights-biases-artifact-upload-failed-3d0e2901618ad3f8))

- **weilanx-creator-blackgold-video**: 「黑金光影风」知识短片一键成片工作流：用户只给一个选题（或者只说「做一期」），就一条龙做完：查一手资料 → 定锚词、写选题与分镜脚本 → 卡 we-never 的拍子写时间表 → 合成音效 → 为选题设计专属画面 → 测试 → 自动抽帧和测响度、自己修 → 直接导出成片 → 代码渲染的 3:4 封面 + 发布文案 + 双语字幕稿，全程不停下来问用户确认。成片是横屏 16:9、约 2 分 18 秒、无口播：近黑底暖色烟雾、一切发光，金粒子聚成标题，霓虹收益矩阵 / 天平 / 对账卡把推理画出来，发光角色在聚光灯下演出模型，底部中英双语字幕，两个爆点踩配乐重拍，片尾停在评论区问题。用户说「黑金风做一期」「黑金光影风」「这期做 XX 博弈 / 悖论 / 效应」「用博弈论讲讲 XX」「做个智猪博弈那样的视频」「一条龙做一期知识视频」时都用这个 skill；只要是用一个模型解释一个反直觉现象（博弈论、经济学、决策、认知偏差、概率、AI 原理）的无口播短片，就用它。 ([Azure12355/weilanx-claude-videos](https://github.com/Azure12355/weilanx-claude-videos/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/weilanx-creator-blackgold-video) · ★276)
- **weilanx-creator-desk-video**: 「暖纸工作台风」口播视频工作流：用户给一个主题（多是「我是怎么用 AI 做 XX 的」「一件事分几步」这类流程拆解），依次写口播稿、复刻音色配音并加速 1.2 倍、转 SRT；或者用户给一段自己录好的真人口播视频，直接转 SRT 并把真人放进左下角出镜小窗做 A-roll / B-roll。在 weilanx-claude-videos 工程里做成横屏 16:9 的口播视频：暖米色点阵纸当工作台（柔光晕 + 漂浮几何），白色窗口卡片和主视觉图形（清单、终端、同心圆、胶片、流程卡）一层层摆上来又滑走，阿里巴巴普惠体粗黑体标题 + 一块朱橙渐变高亮，顶部步骤条随口播推进，右下角像素吉祥物 CC 常驻换表情，字幕在底部；再出 3:4 封面和发布文案，打开 Studio 给用户预览，确认后导出（成片 + 分轨 + 封面 + 字幕稿 + 文案）。用户说「用暖纸风做一期」「做一期流程拆解」「像 dl(4) 那种口播风格做一期」「用工作台风格讲讲 XX」「做一期 XX 分几步」「我录好了口播，用暖纸风做出来」时都用这个 skill。 ([Azure12355/weilanx-claude-videos](https://github.com/Azure12355/weilanx-claude-videos/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/weilanx-creator-desk-video) · ★276)

- **welch-perspective**: 以杰克·韦尔奇的数一数二、活力曲线和变革领导力来分析问题 当用户明确要求使用这一人物的视角、提到“welch-perspective”，或希望应用其核心方法分析问题时使用；不要因一般性问题自动触发。 ([nuwa-skills/welch-skill](https://github.com/nuwa-skills/welch-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **welch-t-test-asynchrony-comparison**: Use when comparing whether peak-gene pairs ranked by a Granger-causal method show significantly longer chromatin-to-expression pseudotime lags than pairs ranked by Pearson or pseudocell correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-asynchrony-comparison))
- **welch-t-test-cell-proportion-testing**: Use when PILOT's Wasserstein-distance-based Leiden clustering has assigned patient samples to clusters (e.g., the PDAC 11 control / 14 Tumor 1 / 10 Tumor 2 split) and you need to test which cell-type proportions differ between clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-cell-proportion-testing))
- **welch-t-test-de-testing**: Use when a clustered single-cell RNA-seq AnnData object needs a per-cluster DE profile to assign a cell type, e.g. testing one Leiden cluster against the rest before GO/KEGG/MSigDB enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-de-testing))
- **welch-t-test-differential-expression**: Use when each cell in a single-cell dataset (scRNA-seq or a scATAC-seq cell-by-gene activity matrix) already has cluster labels and you must identify marker genes per cluster via one-vs-rest testing, ranked by fold-change for marker or enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-differential-expression))
- **welch-t-test-log-transformed-intensities**: Use when comparing log-transformed GC-TOF MS metabolite intensity distributions between two groups of biological metadata triads (organ/species/disease) and you need per-compound p-values with unequal-variance robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-t-test-log-transformed-intensities))
- **welch-t-test-statistical-comparison**: Use when two independent groups of per-pair scores (e.g., APSS from SNP-only vs indel-only Bacmeta simulations) must be compared statistically, and per-point variances or sample sizes may be unequal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/welch-t-test-statistical-comparison))
- **welch-test-log-transformed-intensity-comparison**: Use when comparing metabolite intensities across two broad biological ontology categories (e.g. 'digestive tract' vs 'Bacteria') over many metadata-combination pairs, where unequal variances and log-normal intensity distributions require Welch's t-test on log-transformed values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-test-log-transformed-intensity-comparison))
- **welch-two-sample-t-test-metabolomics**: Use when raw per-metabolite p-values are needed from a two-group comparison (e.g., sex) of ungrouped log-transformed metabolite abundances via Welch's unequal-variance t-tests, prior to eigen-analysis-based FWER adjustment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-two-sample-t-test-metabolomics))
- **welch-two-sample-t-testing**: Use when cells have been assigned intact or mutant gRNAs by GiRAFR and you need to test whether mutant gRNA cells fail to downregulate their target genes relative to intact gRNA cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-two-sample-t-testing))
- **welch-two-sample-ttest-adduct-abundance-comparison**: Use when validating ion-identity/adduct assignments from an aligned MS1 feature table across experimental conditions where a specific adduct class should change in abundance (e.g. post-column salt infusion of sodium acetate or ammonium acetate vs H2O control). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-two-sample-ttest-adduct-abundance-comparison))
- **welchs-t-test-significance-testing**: Use when a putative DEL/DUP call (e.g., from Sniffles/CuteSV/SVIM/NanoVar nanopore pipelines) needs coverage-based validation: supported if coverage shift &gt;=40% with Welch t-test p &lt; 0.05, discarded otherwise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/welchs-t-test-significance-testing))

- **wen-ming-sheng-shuai**: 当用户分析"文明/城市为何盛极而衰""奢侈与技艺积累的双面性"时调用。 关键信号：文明兴衰/奢侈/技艺/由俭入奢。不适用于把文明衰亡归单一道德谴责。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/li-shi-xu-lun/wen-ming-sheng-shuai) · ★1)
- **wen-wang-gua**: The recursive Wen Wang Gua (文王卦) / Yijing interpretive apparatus — the full traditional toolkit for casting and reading a hexagram: Six Relatives (六亲), Na Jia (納甲) line-to-branch mapping, Five Elements (五行) producing/controlling dynamics, palace (卦宫) position, Self and Usage lines (世爻/用神), moving lines and the transformed hexagram, classical line-text hermeneutics, and dramatized Six-Relatives narrative. A method-skill: a menu of interpretive moves to improvise a living reading from, NOT a form to fill out. Use when asked to consult the Yijing, cast a hexagram, run Wen Wang Gua on a situation… ([Wondermonger-daydreaming/claude-skills-library](https://github.com/Wondermonger-daydreaming/claude-skills-library/tree/HEAD/skills/wen-wang-gua) · ★7)
- **wen-xin-diao-long**: 当用户需要"写作方法论、文章结构、文风批评、想象与构思、鉴赏批评"时调用。触发信号：怎么写、文风如何、结构乱、如何鉴赏好文章、想象枯竭。不适用于纯编程/技术文档规范（可借熔裁思路）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/wen-xin-diao-long) · ★1)
- **wen-xin-diao-long-zong-gang**: 《文心雕龙》全书体系总览。当需要在"原道/神思/风骨/熔裁/知音"等多环节间统筹一篇文章的完整写作—批评流程时调用。单点问题请用对应子技能。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/wen-xin-diao-long/wen-xin-diao-long-zong-gang) · ★1)
